nhe47_md_own_structural_pair_valid = {
    exists = scope:actor
    exists = scope:recipient
    scope:actor = scope:recipient
    exists = scope:nhe47_md_a
    exists = scope:nhe47_md_b
    scope:nhe47_md_a = { this != scope:nhe47_md_b owner = scope:actor }
    scope:nhe47_md_b = { owner = scope:actor }
    OR = {
        AND = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { is_domicile_type = estate } scope:nhe47_md_b = { is_domicile_type = estate } }
        AND = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { nhe72_is_east_asian_estate_trigger = yes } scope:nhe47_md_b = { nhe72_is_east_asian_estate_trigger = yes } }
        AND = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { is_domicile_type = japanese_manor } scope:nhe47_md_b = { is_domicile_type = japanese_manor } }
            AND = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { is_domicile_type = camp } scope:nhe47_md_b = { is_domicile_type = camp } }
        AND = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { is_domicile_type = yurt } scope:nhe47_md_b = { is_domicile_type = yurt } }
        AND = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { is_domicile_type = ecclesiastical_domicile } scope:nhe47_md_b = { is_domicile_type = ecclesiastical_domicile } }
    }
}

nhe47_md_core_base_pair_valid = {
    OR = {
        nhe47_md_own_structural_pair_valid = yes
        AND = { scope:actor != scope:recipient
    exists = scope:actor
    exists = scope:recipient
    exists = scope:nhe47_md_a
    exists = scope:nhe47_md_b
    scope:actor = {
        is_alive = yes
        NOT = {
            has_variable = nhe47_md_swap_busy
        }
        NOT = {
            has_variable = nhe47_swap_incomplete
        }
    }
    scope:recipient = {
        is_alive = yes
        NOT = {
            has_variable = nhe47_md_swap_busy
        }
        NOT = {
            has_variable = nhe47_swap_incomplete
        }
    }
    scope:nhe47_md_a = {
        this != scope:nhe47_md_b
        owner = scope:actor
        has_ongoing_domicile_construction = no
    }
    scope:nhe47_md_b = {
        owner = scope:recipient
        has_ongoing_domicile_construction = no
    }
    nhe47_md_pair_japanese_aspirations_valid = yes
    scope:nhe47_md_a = { nhe47_md_core_single_main = yes }
    scope:nhe47_md_b = { nhe47_md_core_single_main = yes }
    OR = {
        AND = {
            scope:nhe47_md_a = {
                is_domicile_type = estate
                num_domicile_buildings = nhe47_md_known_estate_count
            }
            scope:nhe47_md_b = {
                is_domicile_type = estate
                num_domicile_buildings = nhe47_md_known_estate_count
            }
        }
        AND = {
            scope:nhe47_md_a = {
                nhe72_is_east_asian_estate_trigger = yes
                num_domicile_buildings = nhe47_md_known_east_asian_estate_count
            }
            scope:nhe47_md_b = {
                nhe72_is_east_asian_estate_trigger = yes
                num_domicile_buildings = nhe47_md_known_east_asian_estate_count
            }
        }
        AND = {
            scope:nhe47_md_a = {
                is_domicile_type = japanese_manor
                num_domicile_buildings = nhe47_md_known_japanese_manor_count
            }
            scope:nhe47_md_b = {
                is_domicile_type = japanese_manor
                num_domicile_buildings = nhe47_md_known_japanese_manor_count
            }
        }
    }
    scope:actor = { NOT = { has_variable = nhe47_md_core_sync_busy } NOT = { has_variable = nhe47_md_core_sync_failed } }

        }
    }
}

    # Existing buildings may move regardless of original construction innovations.
    always = yes
}
